00222: # Material targeting uses semantic EMPTY descendants, never primitive-name guesses.
00223: def descendants(name):
00224:     marker=bpy.data.objects.get(name)
00225:     if marker is None:
00226:         candidates=[o for o in bpy.data.objects if o.type=='EMPTY' and o.name.startswith(name)]
00227:         assert candidates, name
00228:         marker=candidates[0]
00229:     meshes=[o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00230:     assert meshes, name
00231:     return marker,meshes
00232: 
00233: def fabric_material(name,base,striped=False):
00234:     m=bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes=True
00235:     n=m.node_tree.nodes; l=m.node_tree.links
00236:     bs=n.get('Principled BSDF')
00237:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.88
00238:     tex=n.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00239:     tex.object=bpy.data.objects.get('chaise_longue')
00240:     noise=n.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise'); noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=165
00241:     noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00242:     l.new(tex.outputs['Object'],noise.inputs['Vector'])
00243:     bump=n.new('ShaderNodeBump'); bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=.16
00244:     bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=.008
00245:     l.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00246:     l.new(bump.outputs['Normal'],bs.inputs['Normal'])
00247:     bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value=(*base,1)
00248:     if striped:
00249:         wave=n.new('ShaderNodeTexWave'); wave.wave_type='BANDS'; wave.bands_direction='Y'
00250:         wave.inputs['Scale'].default_value=17
00251:         l.new(tex.outputs['Object'],wave.inputs['Vector'])
00252:         ramp=n.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00253:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.75
00254:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=(*base,1)
00255:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.91
00256:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=(.61,.43,.39,1)
00257:         l.new(wave.outputs['Fac'],ramp.inputs[0])
00258:         l.new(ramp.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00259:     return m

00280: tc=n.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord'); tc.object=marker
00281: waves=[]
